## Malayasia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you.

You mentioned that Vaiṣṇavas never relax. They are always engaged in devotional service twenty-four hours a day, and you said this is actually *samādhi.* Our real fortune is to have *jñāna-sukṛti,* and that is the association of pure, unalloyed devotees like Your Divine Grace. Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Lord Nityānanda Prabhu are like the sun and moon dissipating the illusion of this world. You are certainly empowered by Them to deliver us from birth and death, to take us back home, back to Godhead.

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**"The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord, because he is the most confidential servitor of the Lord. This is acknowledged in all revealed scriptures and followed by all authorities. Therefore I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is a bona fide representative of Śrī Hari \[Kṛṣṇa\]."**

You mentioned that you came across the purport written by Bhaktivinoda that the order of the spiritual master is the life and soul of the disciple. "My only wish is to have my consciousness purified by the words emanating from his lotus mouth. Attachment to his lotus feet is the perfection that fulfills all desires." This gave you the inspiration to fully surrender to your spiritual master without any reservation. You mentioned that every word you speak is coming from your spiritual master, and that is how ISKCON is pure and powerful. You mentioned that ISKCON maintains purity because you have built temples in holy *dhāmas* such as Vṛndāvana and Māyāpur. All our full-time ISKCON centers and the homes of initiated devotees who are practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness are places of pilgrimage.

Even we may not remember Kṛṣṇa very much, but by serving and remembering more about your devotional activities and hearing from you the glorification of Kṛṣṇa, we can advance and remain spiritually staunch. You also mentioned on the cover *of Back to Godhead* that Godhead is light and nescience is darkness, and by this principle the movement started. Your mercy is like the cloud pouring water to destroy the forest fire of illusion.

Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu said that a Vaiṣṇava is seen by his association with devotees and dissassociation with nondevotees. So ISKCON is a body of *sādhu-saṅga* and a home in which the whole world can live in. The test of a spiritual personality is seen by his humility, sacrifice, quality, and pious activities. Only by taking full shelter of your lotus feet can we remain as pure spiritual personalities.

Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu prophesied that Kṛṣṇa consciousness will spread all over the world. If we do not preach, somebody else will take the credit.

As stated in the *Bhagavad-gītā,* "The blazing fire turns firewood to ashes. O Arjuna, so does the fire of knowledge turn to ashes all reaction to material activities." Similarly, your fire of preaching is the best medicine to burn to ashes the impersonalist and voidist concepts.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you said that ultimately the devotee has to prepare himself for the *sannyāsa* order and be completely detached from all material attachments. If one denies the *sannyāsa* order, then one's spiritual progress is hampered. Your Divine Grace renounced country, home, money, and family and preached in a spirit of detachment.

Praying at your lotus feet that there should not be any impediments in ISKCON's progress, we beg to remain serving you as your humble servants and as you desire.

(Written by Bhaktivrajendranandana Swami)

*Your eternally indebted servants of ISKCON Malaysia,*

## Manchester

Dear Prabhupāda, we come once more  
to catch with praise thy qualities,  
in hope that we might somehow please  
thee and thus thy most dear Lord.

To rightly mark this great event  
demands a gathering of such size  
that all the worlds doth not comprise,  
offering praise and testaments.

Still we, the few united here,  
offer prayers this sacred day,  
that thou might show to us the way  
to swell our number year by year.

Brilliant sun that blazed across  
the darkened sky and in its course  
burned to ash with fiery force  
the growing hordes of godless dross,

Thy truth resounds more loudly still  
with the passing of every day,  
and with it shall thou ever stay,  
to guide our weak and wandering will.

The greatest gift you freely gave;  
we stand eternally in debt,  
yet blessed in full our one regret:  
the lost time we were not thy slave.

What hope for we who'd chosen pain?  
Decisions born of ignorance,  
to place us in defiant stance  
against the love you show again.

But now from thee a life renewed,  
to us, we cannot fathom why,  
it rests with us to simply try  
and fetch thee to the multitude.

The task complete by thy strong will,  
but left to us to work with time,  
which will alone show thy design  
and cure the universal ill.

Expanding sea of causeless love  
that bursts the dam of time and space,  
to wash away our long disgrace  
and all our faithless fears remove.  
From out that sea a golden wave  
hath gently lapped into our hearts,  
nor will not ever yet depart;  
we had but faults, but thou forgave.

We the foolish, we the wretched,  
we the fallen, yet so proud,  
though soon by nature to be bowed,  
are raised by pity's hand outstretched.

More widely shall thy glories range  
than any man that yet hath trod  
upon the earth's mistreated sod  
and brought about such wondrous change.

In truth thou wrought such massive good  
that poet's pen might not describe,  
that time alone shall just ascribe,  
to thee thy glories' magnitude.

Expressing feelings hardly felt,  
we pray they one day pierce our hearts,  
and that we have in some small part  
in truth on thy great glory dwelt.

Thus falling helpless at thy feet,  
we offer here our paltry praise,  
one tiny flower that swiftly fades,  
as thou dost all our words defeat.

O lord and master, your fallen servants in Manchester are praying for a drop of your boundless mercy that we might be successful in our insignificant preaching attempt. You are our religion, the goal of our religious practices, and our only hope of liberation. Please glance lovingly upon us.

*Your servants,*

Kṛṣṇa-dharma dāsa / Govardhana-seva dāsa / Abhaya dāsa / Guru-dakṣiṇa dāsa / Bhakta Captain / Yaśasvinī-devī dāsī / Cintāmaṇi-dhāma-devī dāsī / Śrīdhāmā-devī dāsī / Vratanī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Valda

## Mauritius

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Your appearance in this mortal world is the most significant event in the life of a devotee. Just as when Lord Kṛṣṇa appeared, all kinds of demoniac forces were destroyed, similarly when you appeared with your ISKCON movement the atheistic forces of *nirviśeṣa* and *śūnyavādī* retreated, and false religionists and pseudo-devotees were lashed with the whip of your Bhaktivedanta purports. They could no longer perform their activities openly. Your appearance established order in the field of *dharma.*

When you left our association we all feared the worst, because you had already predicted what happens after the *ācārya* disappears: "Unfortunately, when the *ācārya* disappears rogues and nondevotees take advantage and immediately begin to introduce unauthorized principles in the name of so-called *svāmīs, yogīs,* philanthropists, welfare workers, and so on." (purport to *Bhāg.* 4.26.48)

As your ISKCON movement passes through its most intense difficulties, we have to stick fanatically to the way you set up the process of devotional service. "Do as I do" becomes our guiding light, because the way you did things is the right way. If we always do as you did, how can we ever deviate? It is not possible. But as soon as we introduce our own path, then we move into the danger zone.

In the same purport you write: "The *ācārya,* the authorized representative of the Supreme Lord, establishes these principles (as mentioned in Bg. 9.34), but when he disappears things once again become disordered. The perfect disciples of the *ācārya* try to relieve the situation by sincerely following the instructions of the spiritual master."

Your sincere disciples are trying very hard to reestablish order in the ISKCON world. They need your guidance, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please manifest clear understanding of the principles of devotional service and Vaiṣṇava behavior in their hearts.

We pray to you to always let us remain vigilant to protect your movement against those who want to destroy it. You have already warned us, "These people may come even in the disguise *of svāmīs* and *yogīs."* Give us the strength to keep your ISKCON intact by strictly following the way you told us to: "Everyone should cooperate with this movement in order to bring about actual peace and happiness in the world." *(Bhāg.* 4.28.48)

You are requesting the people of the world to cooperate with us. Therefore, how much more do *we* have to cooperate amongst ourselves. That was also your last wish before leaving us. Unfortunately, cooperation has been treated in ISKCON like an "untouchable" practically ever since you left the planet.

We are sure that if all the members of ISKCON put away their personal desires for name, fame, and distinction, we can all work together nicely and satisfy you by again becoming a potent preaching force in the world. Disciples are not the force; wealth is not the force; fame is not the force; a big zone is not the force; scholarship is not the force; PURITY IS THE FORCE.

We are quarreling amongst ourselves, but then on your appearance day we offer flowers at your *vyāsāsana* and deliver big speeches. What is the use of all this if we cannot cooperate amongst each other? We are sure you have seen through that scam long ago.

It reminds us very much of the situation that existed on Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s Vyāsa-pūjā in 1961, when you wrote that,

simply a festival of flowers and fruits does not constitute worship. The one who serves the message of the *guru* really worships him. Oh, shame! My dear brothers, aren't you embarrassed? In the manner of businessmen you increase your disciples. But just take a good look at the situation that has arisen. Everyone has become a sense enjoyer and has given up preaching... . . . Make your promise today. Give up all your politics and diplomacy.

Our humble request today is that you guide us out of our gross ignorance that has enveloped us for some time and make us realize the essence of your instructions. Let us not be deviated from your service by desires for prestige, followers, wealth, women, etc. Let us always keep your *vāṇī* foremost on our minds. Then the meaning of Vyāsa-pūjā will be truly fulfilled.

*Your insignificant disciples and granddisciples in Mauritius,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Arjuna dāsa / Śrīniketana dāsa

## Melbourne

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Just as the cloud pours water on the forest fire to extinguish it, similarly, by your soothing, raineloudlike presence, you are extinguishing the fire of lust blazing in our hearts.

Chanting the holy names, dancing in ecstasy, singing, and playing upon musical instruments, you are teaching us by your divine example how to relish the mellows of pure devotion at the lotus feet of the holy name. When will the tears flow from our eyes like waves?

You are always engaged in worshiping Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and instructing us how to follow, by dressing the Deities in beautiful clothes and ornaments and rendering humble service by cleansing the temple of the Lord. Thus our hearts are gradually becoming cleansed.

You are regularly offering Kṛṣṇa the four types of delicious foodstuffs prepared by your disciples with love and devotion. When Kṛṣṇa is satisfied, and when the devotees honor the *mahā-prasādam,* they are satisfied. Let the whole world become satisfied by serving you.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are always eager to hear and chant about the qualities, names, and forms of the conjugal pastimes of Rādhikā and Mādhava. Please give us a drop of that taste for hearing and chanting! Help us to aspire for these things at every moment!

You are always absorbed in expertly assisting the *gopīs,* who at different times make different tasteful arrangements for conjugal loving affairs in the groves of Vṛndāvana. Yet you are so kind as to simultaneously tend to us, your wayward servants.

All the great authorities declare your glories. You are the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead. You are honored as much as the Lord Himself, because you are his bona fide representative.

By your grace, Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us the mercy of Kṛṣṇa. Without pleasing you, we cannot make any advancement. Let us always remember your lotus feet and never forget them. Let all the rules and regulations be the servants of these two principles.

(Written by Kūrma dāsa)

*Let us remain your humble servants, the devotees of Melbourne Mandir,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Miami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Jaya Prabhupāda! Please accept our humble obeisances millions of times unto your blessed lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, for you have come and delivered us from the darkness of ignorance.

We pray that we may always be fixed in assisting you in your divine mission; as it is clearly stated in the scriptures, it is not at all possible for one to understand the sublime nature of the transcendental activities of a *mahā-bhāgavata* like Your Divine Grace. But by your mercy alone, one who has dedicated himself to carrying out your orders as his life and soul immediately becomes empowered to act as your bona fide representative and expand the mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu on your behalf.

You wrote in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.15.13purport): "The Lord is all-powerful, and by His grace His pure devotee can be as powerful as He may desire, and there is not limit to it."

You are the savior of the world; you are a transcendental person, greatest amongst the great. We are eternally indebted to you for engaging us in Kṛṣṇa's service and for giving us authorized versions of the *śāstras* to study and learn about Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us so much facility—temples, books, pastimes, *prasādam,* and endless other opportunities to be Kṛṣṇa conscious and to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura teaches, *yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādaḥ:* "The mercy of the spiritual master makes one eligible to receive Kṛṣṇa's mercy." Only the spiritual master can give any opportunity to serve Kṛṣṇa.

"You live forever by your divine instructions, and the follower lives with you." Please Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us remain always completely absorbed in the printing and distribution of your books, not only in this life but birth after birth. Jaya Prabhupāda!

*Your eternal servants,*

## Miami Beach

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most fallen obeisances in the sacred dust of your lotus feet. On this day of great rejoicing—your holy appearance anniversary—we beg to offer a short prayer for your pleasure.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
May the blazing sun of your words,  
Which enlighten the hearts of all who hear or read them, which dim to invisibility the arguments of the firefly like materialistic and impersonalistic speculators, and which illumine the path back home to Kṛṣṇa for us blind souls wandering in the wilderness of the Kali-yuga,  
Forever shine in this world.  
May the nectar-river of your mercy,  
Which revives the conditioned souls dying of thirst for lack of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which soothes the hearts of all of us suffering the burning threefold miseries, and which is flowing to every comer of this planet through millions of your books in dozens of languages,  
Forever flow in this world.  
May the moonshine of your love for Kṛṣṇa,  
Which shines so brightly from your heart and moonlike face, which expands to embrace all living entities, parts and parcels of your beloved, which makes the high tide of auspiciousness rise and inundate this parched and unfortunate planet with the ocean of prema, and which nourishes the fragile plants of your devotees, who draw strength and faith therefrom,  
Forever flow within this world.  
May the cintāmaṇi gems of your transcendental pastimes,  
Which amaze even the demigods in heaven with their luster, which reflect in millions of ways your unlimited exalted qualities—such as humility, compassion, and boldness in preaching the message of Lord Caitanya—and which, by their self-effulgence, reveal you to be an intimate associate of Kṛṣṇa, empowered to spread His teachings all over the universe,  
Forever enrich this world.  
May the lotus flowers of your sincere followers,  
Who spread the sweet fragrance of your teachings far and wide by distributing your books, who are firmly rooted in the ground of the philosophy of Bhagavad-gītā, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, and the other books you gave us, and whose multitude of transcendental qualities—foremost of which is their faithfulness to you—continually attract thousands more to take shelter of your lotus feet,  
Forever beautify this world.

May the good ship of your ISKCON,  
Which you so painstakingly built plank by plank, bolt by bolt, of which you are the eternal captain, the founder-ācārya, and on the decks of which all souls can find shelter from the stormy ocean of birth and death,  
Forever remain seaworthy and on course in its journey to Kṛṣṇaloka.

*Jagad-guru Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!*

*Your servants in Miami Beach,*

## Murwillumbah, Australia

O Son of Sarasvatī,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
your preaching mood  
forceful and hard;  
one hand a club,  
a Bhāgavatam the other,  
your weapon of truth  
our nonsense to smother.

Your words are eternal,  
so who can say  
that you are not here  
guiding ISKCON today?  
Our course is not charted  
by meetings or men  
but by your teachings  
again and again.

As much as we hear  
from your lotus mouth,  
that much our hearts  
grow strong and devout,  
and though we may see  
a storm on the ocean,  
your followers, like fish,  
are safe in devotion.

The house that you built  
will never diminish;  
the work you started  
will never be finished;  
your mission of mercy  
for all fallen souls  
is our most cherished  
and sacred of goals.

We look for security  
in mundane spheres,  
but all we find  
is misery and tears.  
Please keep us secure  
under your lotus feet,  
and then, by your grace  
our Lord we will meet.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Jagad-guru,  
so proud you are  
of Mahāprabhu.  
You are His whip,  
His man for this age;  
our pride is for you  
evangelical sage.

Your kīrtanas come straight  
from the spiritual sky  
to water our hearts,  
so thirsty and dry.  
Lord Kṛṣṇa comes  
whenever you call  
because your heart is soft,  
the purest of all.

O master, please  
be kind unto us.  
At times we are covered  
by anger and lust,  
but we have no shelter  
beside Your Divine Grace,  
and our pledge is to serve you  
the rest of our days.

*ALL GLORIES TO YOU, ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!*

## Nava Jiyada-nṛsiṁha-kṣetra

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Your glories are as unlimited as the waves of the ocean.  
Who can count them?  
The powerful waves of your mercy will continue to flood  
the barren land of repeated birth and death.  
Who can stop them?  
You planted innumerable seeds in that barren land.  
Many have come to sprout, some have grown to trees,  
some are bearing flowers, and others are still hidden.  
Who can find them?  
You showed many wonders.  
Those who love you see the miracles only you could do and  
the master pulling the strings to your unparalleled actions.  
Who can see Him?  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, your deep compassion for the suffering souls  
cannot be compared to anything.  
Please let a drop of it remain in this hopeless world.  
And let us show those souls the path to your lotus feet!

## New Gauḍadeśa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a saint.  
With quill in hand you came not to paint  
A picture of this life as the goal  
But drew us the map to take us home.

So many cheaters have come with their wares,  
But all of their magic can't chase away fears,  
For the problems we have can't be saved by a crook,  
So you came to our side with the bhāgavatam book.  
You are such a friend that you took the risk  
And came to this world and possibly missed  
The chance to sport freely with the Lord of your heart,  
The Lord of Vṛndāvana, and the rāsa dance.

But would your Lord dare to forget you outright  
And leave you alone in the material night?  
No, I would say He was walking with you,  
Filling your heart with jays ever new.

For when you were faced with a trial or a test  
You surrendered to it and did what came best.  
You spoke of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Lord in your heart,  
And you showed Him to us and you gave us our start.

Your body was old but your spirit was fresh,  
Just like a spring chick out of the nest.  
Tirelessly working you showed us each day  
How to please Kṛṣṇa the bhakti way.

You 're the greatest preacher ever to come.  
Prabhupāda, you are second to none,  
For in all the scriptures that speak what is true,  
There is no one described who equals you.  
Your servants at New Gauḍadeśa (Australia),

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Krodheśvara dāsa

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## New Orleans

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respected obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, we want to thank you for all the wonderful things you have given us. *You* have solved all of our problems. There might be bumps and rumbles around us, but internally, as long as we are fixed on you, there is no problem.

You have truly removed the word *impossible* from our lives, and with it the shackles of material illusion.

Now we can ride into battle with Kṛṣṇa and Arjuna, we can fly past the heavens with Pṛthu and Queen Arci into the spiritual realms. We can dive deep into the ocean with Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma and visit the kingdom of Varuṇa. We can sit on the bank of the Ganges with the greatest of sages and hear the *Bhāgavatam* sung.

Yes, an unlimited ever-expanding supply of wonderful adventures and pastimes that have become our rightful claim if we take shelter of you.

Thank you again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and please bless us that we always be true to you and your ISKCON, because they are one and the same.

(Written by Jvarajit dāsa)

*Your servants in New Orleans,*

## New Talavana

Śrīla Prabhupāda, Lord Gauranga 's angel sublime!  
Your beauty transcends both matter and time.  
Your teaching so perfect, your form like a swan's,  
To the doomed "modem" world, you came as a new dawn.

You showed us our souls, and the original form of God.  
You revealed the highest path which the mahājanas have trod.  
With spiritual food, you nurtured us to health,  
And you showed us so boldly that austerity is wealth.  
You traveled and gave yourself freely to all,  
Writing and instructing in English, answering Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta's call.  
No one can measure the service you rendered,  
For you melted our hearts with your full surrender,  
The holy name was your gift, a matchless one beyond compare.  
The Western world would not have heard of "Hare Kṛṣṇa " had you not been there.

The clouds of Kali darken; mankind rushes toward the brink.  
Oh, if only from the cup of your kindness they would drink!  
Give us your mercy, let us stand in your light;  
To preach Gaurāṅga’s message let us follow you and fight.

It will all be your grace, not by anything we can do,  
But we must work hard 'til our life here is through.  
Then we pray to fall at your lotus feet in the spiritual sky,  
Chanting there together "Śrīla Prabhupāda Ki Jaya!"

(Written by Caturbāhu dāsa)

*Your servants in New Tālavana, Mississippi,*

## New Vrajamaṇḍala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear ŚrīIa Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. Today is the most important day of the year, because in order to celebrate your appearance day, we have come together here to glorify and remember you. To honor the genuine representative of Vyāsa is one of the most important events in human life and bestows all good fortune to the conditioned souls. To be able to worship and remember a pure devotee of the Lord is the most desirable thing in the three worlds, because by the mercy of a pure devotee like Your Divine Grace, even lower creatures like ourselves can attain the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, who bears a flute in His hands and wears a peacock feather in His hair. This is confirmed by Prahlāda Mahārāja as follows:

*naiṣāṁ matis tāvad urukramāṅghriṁ*  
*spṛśaty anarthāpagamo yad-arthaḥ*  
*mahīyasāṁ pāda-rajo 'bhiṣekaṁ*  
*niṣkiñcanānāṁ na vṛṇīta yāvat*

**"Unless they smear upon their bodies the dust of the lotus feet of a Vaiṣṇava completely freed from material contamination, persons very much inclined toward materialistic life cannot be attached to the lotus feet of the Lord, who is glorified for His uncommon activities. Only by becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious and taking shelter at the lotus feet of the Lord in this way can one be freed from material contamination."**

It is very difficult to find a pure devotee of the Lord in this world, but you are so merciful that you have made this seemingly so difficult task very easy by coming to the Western world and giving yourself to everybody in the form of your books. People from all parts of the world are now able to receive your blessings and association by reading your wonderful books, and thus they are able to taste the nectar of the lotus feet of the Lord. That is your mercy. Your compassion is so great that words cannot describe it. With these thoughts in mind, we feel very tiny and insignificant, and we feel eternally indebted to you. The only way we can try to repay a small fraction of this debt is to try to help and serve you in your mission. We want to make your instructions our life and soul. We want to be your instruments by distributing your books and by teaching everyone what you have taught us. We want to mold our lives according to your instructions and in this way be living examples. Then there is hope that the sublime message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness will enter into everyone's heart, purify it, and make it suitable to receive Kṛṣṇa. That is our desire. This is how we want to serve and worship you. Please, empower us so that we can assist you in this way. Whatever we do, whatever we have, whatever we say, whatever we think is done properly, is only due to your divine mercy. Therefore, we pray that we may always remember you and thus be able to "do as you did."

Begging again and again for your blessings,  
*Your insignificant servants at New Vrajamaṇḍala, Spain,*

## New York

Dear ŚrīIa Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this most auspicious of days, we celebrate your appearance within the material world just to deliver all the fallen souls of Kali-yuga. Of course, we cannot easily grasp the magnitude of this compassion you are showing, symbolized in you appearance day. Yet we are trying to begin to appreciate that compassion by meditating on your appearance and constant presence in our lives, remaking our lives, reforming our lives, from lives of certain doom to hopeful, meaningful, progressive, and God-centered lives.

We meditate on your presence as we read your glorious Bhaktivedanta purports. If we read with a little understanding, this is you speaking the eternal truths to us.

We meditate on your presence as we see the smile of realization on the face of a new devotee hearing one of your perfect analogies for the first time. This is you smiling sweetly on us.

We meditate on your presence when we receive our due reaction from the material energy after neglecting your instructions. This is you mercifully chastising us.

We meditate on your presence when we feel some consolation in the concern of our loving Godbrothers. This is you encouraging us.

We meditate on your presence when we feel satisfaction through some small accomplishment on behalf of your mission. This is you reciprocating with us.

Every moment is an opportunity to meditate on your presence. We pray for such constant meditation, as it would certainly be our lives' perfection.

(Written by Bhaktarūpa dāsa) *Your servants,*

## Nigeria

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Our humble obeisances are unto you. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, who are dear to Sarasvatī Gosvāmī.  
We offer our gratitude to you for establishing ISKCON so that we can take shelter under its umbrella.  
We thank you for giving us expert guides who are very dear to us.  
We glorify you for blessing the whole world with the *Bhagavad-gītā.*  
We sing your praises for inundating the nescient regions with the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* the cream of Vedic knowledge and the last word in transcendence.  
For these and many other reasons, we shall remain ever grateful to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
All glories to you again and again!

## North Brazil

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Cāṇakya Paṇḍita said, "A king is respected only inside his kingdom, while a saint is respected all over the world."

Your advice, your books, your ISKCON—O Śrīla Prabhupāda, how many unforgettable things you have left for us! Please now give us your mercy so that we can maintain them. They are certainly the only salvation for the whole world, which is caught in the ignorance of Kali-yuga.

Only those who do not devīate from your basic instructions are able to maintain their spiritual lives and broadcast the pure glories of your International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

*vyavasāyātmikā buddhir*  
*ekeha kuru-nandana*  
*bahu-śākhā hy anantāś ca*  
*buddhayo 'vyavasāyinām*

**"Those who are on this path are resolute in their purpose, and their aim is one. O beloved son of the Kurus, the intelligence of those who are irresolute is many-branched." (Bg. 2.41)**

We can understand your purpose by reading your books, and we can be sure that your aim always was one—to maintain ISKCON's unity. But many divisions have taken place because of those with many-branched intelligence. If you were here personally, how many of your disciples would feel ashamed and ask your forgiveness? Why don't they do that now? Why don't they understand that you are with us in your instructions and that very soon, by following them, we would all be situated together near you?

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, protect us! We are all your young children assuming responsible positions. Please forgive us. On this day, let us be reminded that you are in the hearts of those who follow you.

(Written by Jagad-vicitra dāsa)

*Your servants, the devotees from Recife, Natal, Fortaleza, Salvador, Belem, Maraus, and Vraja Maṇḍala Bhūmi Farm.*

## Panama

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We can't even imagine the great mercy of having such a great father as you. If it hadn't been for you, we would still be chewing the chewed and living a life foil of illusion and frustration.

Soft as a rose with the humble and meek and harder than a thunderbolt with the atheists and demons—O blessed Śrīla Prabhupāda! When all seemed lost, you were always there to remind us of Kṛṣṇa and kept the ship aloft from the towering waves of *māyā.* But that same spirit of hope and faith still lives on with us in ISKCON, because you showed us how to do it, with your life, your example, and teachings.

Thank you once again, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Thank you for your matchless gifts. We can't even imagine the great mercy of having such a great father as you. Nor will there ever be anyone greater than you.

*Your most fallen servants of Panama Mandira,*

## Penang

**You are the only one**

When all is said and done,  
You are the only one.

When all accusations are laid to rest,  
You remain the best.

Your message means no confusion;  
Your words alone dispel all illusion.

Unmotivated, uninterrupted, your service divine,  
No concept of "I," "me," or "mine."

Let us be in some small way like you,  
Doing as you would have us do.

Never minimizing what you have taught  
To satisfy some ambitious thought.

Your desires enough to circumvent  
Any signs of discontent.

Prabhupāda, your mercy is our saving grace;  
Our faith in you can't be replaced.

Crown jewel on the head of this world  
That you are—let none doubt our word.

For when all is said and done,  
You are the only one,  
Bhaktisiddhānta’s true champion.  
Let us continue to champion your cause eternally.

*Your humble servants in Penang State.*

## Perth

"Soon there will appear a personality who will preach the holy name of Hari all over the world" –Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura

Five hundred years ago the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, appeared and spread the chanting of the holy name throughout India. Through disciplic succession the holy name was passed down to the present day. In 1965, however, a remarkable occurrence took place when the holy name and teachings of Lord Caitanya were spread beyond India and took root in the Western countries, where atheism and materialism were rampant. Soon the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra* was to be heard in every country of the world. Historically this is a very significant event and will be remembered for many thousands of years to come as the beginning of a worldwide revival of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda will also be remembered and worshiped as that great personality responsible.

We learn from the *Bhagavad-gītā* (4.7) that whenever there is a decline in religious practice and a rise of irreligion, the Supreme Lord personally descends, and sometimes He sends His empowered representatives. His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda is one of those divinely empowered representatives coming in disciplic succession from Kṛṣṇa and purely presenting His teachings and the path to His lotus feet. What's more, he has successfully spread Kṛṣṇa's name and teachings throughout the whole world, even in dark places like Russia. The world owes him a great debt because in these troubled times Kṛṣṇa consciousness is the only remedy to bring about world peace and unity. Hence it is said of Śrīla Prabhupāda that "He built a house in which the whole world can live."

Let us therefore take this opportunity to glorify Śrīla Prabhupāda and pray to be always taking shelter under his divine lotus feet, which are able to give protection to the whole world.

(Written by Puruṣottama dāsa)

*Your servants in Perth, Australia,*

## Philadelphia

Trials and tribulations  
An unending series of tests.  
Blinded by material desires,  
I've ignored what is best.  
Impurities rising from my heart,  
As you apply the heat—  
When will I seriously  
Take shelter at your lotus feet?

Empowered emissary of the Lord,  
Your mercy is beyond comprehension.  
Only the most fortunate souls  
Get a glimpse of your exalted position.  
Losing sight of your love for us,  
We are headed towards defeat.  
The material ocean is easily crossed  
When we accept the boat of your lotus feet.

Is it really such a calamity  
That you are no longer here?  
Your vāṇī is readily available,  
Yet my heart is filled with fear.  
Internal and external problems,  
ISKCON is feeling the heat.  
Still the only shelter–  
Taking the dust of your lotus feet.

Our foolishness haunts us still;  
Let us all take a humble seat,  
Aspiring for your mercy  
In the shade of your lotus feet.  
Love and trust are the keys to success  
If ISKCON's mission is to be complete.  
Let your good sons teach by example  
And gently massage your lotus feet.

We must perfect our sādhana-bhakti  
Lest we submit to māyā's call.  
Our relationships must undergo reform  
Before we take a fall.  
Sādhu-saṅga is our medicine  
That tastes so bitter-sweet.  
You are present within your followers  
Who worship your lotus feet.

You have so much still to teach us,  
But do we want to hear?  
Your books, your association–  
Mankind's solace for the next ten thousand years.  
We do not take advantage of your presence,  
Our illusion is so complete.  
By your mercy only can we remain  
Under the protection of your lotus feet.

The essence of your order:  
Become guru and save this land.  
An impossible mission made easy  
By the grace of your helping hand.  
Let us follow in your footsteps;  
Imitation will cause defeat.  
We are sure to be successful  
By holding tight to your lotus feet.

*(Written Rāmanātha-sukha dāsa)*

*Your servants in Philadelphia,*

## Philippines

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. We are very much indebted to Your Divine Grace for allowing us to participate in Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement.

You have explained to us that to become a devotee means to declare war on *māyā.* While she is inflicting the threefold miseries on conditioned souls, we are countering her attacks with efforts in three directions:

1\. Book Distribution. We are maintaining a stock of all your books. Not only large hardbound titles like *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* but also small and inexpensive publications. Every devotee, without exception, takes time to preach and to distribute your books.

2\. *Prasādam* Distribution. For low-income and indigent persons we have a popular Food for Life program, serving over two thousand meals weekly in Manila and thousands more in other locations. For the higher class we have an office catering program, vegetarian banquets, and cooking demonstrations, all giving more and more people the opportunity to accept the mercy of the Lord.

3\. Holy Name Distribution. As well as regular street *saṅkīrtana* in cities where we have an established center, we are also establishing *"nāma-haṭṭa saṅgas"* in locations where there are no full-time devotees. Records are kept on all interested persons, and they are regularly visited and taught to chant *japa* and to establish altars in their homes. At every opportunity we are holding congregational programs for such interested persons and giving them all opportunity to progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

In the U.S.A. you humbly submitted to your disciples, "This is your country. You know best how to preach here." Here in the Philippines we are seeing many qualified persons coming forward and accepting this preaching mission on your behalf. This is certainly all due to your causeless mercy.

On this auspicious day we pray that we will always remain strict followers in your footsteps.

*Your unworthy servants in the Philippine Yātrā.*

## Poona

All glories to this day,  
Savior of Kali-yuga,  
His Divine Grace.

The West and the East,  
Are suffering the same  
Impersonalism and voidism.  
To rescue them—  
His Divine Grace.  
The difficult days have come now  
To tell us the truth of love for you, Prabhupāda.  
The pushing of the waves of your love  
is rescuing the sincere souls.

Your mercy in display  
is giving chance to all,  
but few are taking advantage  
of such a great soul.

The days are telling us  
how to become your servant,  
without expectation for profit, name, and fame,  
and surrender to you only to serve.

Adore today the great soul Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
the ambassador of the spiritual world.  
What is there to gain  
outside the mercy of His Divine Grace?  
All wretched souls, for how long  
do you want to wander without his mercy  
in this material world?

Let me take shelter in him.  
Do not search for anybody else  
As proposed to you  
By this māyā-saṁsāra.

Keep my master as only one—  
His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda.

The proposals are many  
how to forget him.  
But please, master, enliven me  
and don't let me go to ruin.  
O master, the doubts are hunting me  
and keeping me down,  
begging for the mercy of this saṁsāra.  
Without seeing you  
as master and guide,  
let all the calamities come and go,  
but never forget my master and lord.

Lamenting for my master  
is not only what he wants.  
Let everyone get ready  
to go on saṅkīrtana.

Preaching is your mission  
that brought us together.  
Śrī Caitanya wants us to help you  
to spread His message.

This day of your appearance  
we all shall share with those  
who don't have your mercy and  
don't know the goal of life.

Crying in the wilderness,  
your shelter I don't find.  
You are with the conditioned soul  
in hari-nāma saṅkīrtana.

*Jai Śrīla Prabhupāda!*

(Written by Jayadhvaja Swami)

## Port Harcourt

Dear ŚrīIa Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble and most respectful obeisances. All glories to this uncommon auspicious appearance day of yours. By the time you came to Africa, we were still battling with *māyā,* of which it always seems to us a lost battle. Years after you planted the seed of *sanātana-dharma,* our love for you and your service still remained dormant, due to the influence *of māyā.*

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*

It is only the most foolish who, once the seed of devotional service is planted, fail to either water it or clear away the weeds.

Even though it was clear to you that we were deliriously pursuing mundane things and there was no particle of desire in us to worship *guru* and Kṛṣṇa, still you made your servants available to captain the ship of deliverance that is navigating the ocean of *saṁsāra,* and whoever faithfully and submissively engages in their service will easily cross beyond it.

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*prakāśante mahatmanaḥ*

**"To those great souls who engage in exclusive devotional service to the Supreme Lord and also similarly serve the spiritual master in such pure devotion, all the purports of the Vedic literature are revealed."**

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, engage our minds, souls, and bodies in your devotional service, so that we can come to the platform of realizing how exalted you

(Written by Mahānanda dāsa)

## Puerto Rico

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my fallen obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

I would like to offer you my heartfelt gratitude for giving us good hope of escaping from the clutches of material illusion and returning to the absolute plane of eternality, bliss, and knowledge in association with the all-attractive Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and His eternal consort, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. Your only request is that we remain loyal to you by performing the activities you have given and refraining from the four pillars of sinful life. Naturally you also expect us to make our utmost attempt to deliver your teachings in *paramparā* and thus awaken so many others from their profound slumber.

For myself, I can only stammer in your presence. Never mind your "invisibility "—your mercy is all that I am made of. Your disappearance only applies to the material, bounded context of the three modes of physical nature. Your disciples and followers still want to get your *darśana* and still hope to please you and to become satisfied in your pleasure. It is we who are the "invisible" ones, shrouded in our lifeless bodily robes. "Just as a person puts on new garments, giving up the old and useless ones, similarly the soul accepts new material bodies, giving up the old ones." We become completely mystified by misidentification with these temporary coverings. You kindly awaken us from such ignorance by cutting out our *anarthas—*those dirty things in our hearts that bind us to the process of birth and death.

We are going through a collective purification. This is no time for anything that smacks of complacency. Urgently, we have to chant purely. Urgently, we have to cling to your desires for us to distribute books, to attend *maṅgala arotika,* to learn to become more and more free from the useless habit of material sense gratification. Moreover, you urge us to scrutinizingly *study* your books. That would keep us spiritually fit to deal with *māyā’s* various types of attacks, some subtle, some gross, some directed at an individual, and some against our very existence as a worldwide transcendental society.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give me your mercy. What is the use of my "management"? I cannot even manage myself, nor do I manage to even remember who I really am. My body is getting older so quickly—it has already doubled in age since I met you in 1966. What will happen?

I wish I could remember you always. I feel so useless, apparently separated from Your Divine Grace. Let everyone take shelter in the shade of your lotus feet. Let everyone place the dust of your lotus feet on their heads and make their existence truly auspicious. And let us make your mercy available, ever increasingly, for by distributing your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement we will never lack.

You are the sweetest personality. We never doubted that the Supreme Personality of Godhead Himself is completely pleased with you. Kṛṣṇa is so kind to have sent you, and you, the one-hundred-percent unalloyed servant of Kṛṣṇa, are so kind to have come to save us. You enable us to love and understand Kṛṣṇa, as much as we can accept Him. *Guru* is heavy—he offers God himself to fallen, wretched souls in Kali-yuga. Are we ready to deal with such weight? Or do we prefer to loiter in this temporary, dangerous, and miserable place? One way or the other, we have to cope with Kṛṣṇa's supreme dominion over all that be. Otherwise we foolishly deride Him, only to be crushed by death *(mṛtyuḥ sarva-haraś cāham!).*

You have always been cognizant of everything essential. You teach us of Lord Caitanya's saving grace in the face of the imminent threat of nuclear holocaust. You gave us the peace formula, just as Kṛṣṇa stated: He is the supreme enjoyer, He is the supreme controller, and He is the supreme friend. Who knows how much longer these configurations of material energy will continue intact—these bodies, buildings, streets, cities, countries, etc.? But Kṛṣṇa and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are our eternal friends—Kṛṣṇa and *guru.* There is no other shelter.

Another year has passed. And some tests have been passed as well. All I can do is beg: Śrīla Prabhupāda, please allow me to continue to remain in your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, despite my remaining disqualifications. I know I must become more determined. *You* are sending nice devotees, new and old, to help us serve you. Let me become also nice, beautified by your association. Frog can become prince, worm can transform into butterfly. Blind men can traverse mountains; dumb ones can coherently announce the glories of the Lord. So you have appeared on this day. Let us take strength and renew our remembrance of you as well as our realization of your perpetual presence.

Please forgive my offenses. I only want to please you. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

(Written by Prabhupāda dāsa Brahmacārī)

*Your humble servants of the Puerto Rico Yātrā,*

## Rio de Janeiro

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humblest obeisances at your lotus feet, which can purify the whole universe.

All masters sit down by your feet, for you are Prabhupāda, and the controllers of the universe wait on these feet, for you are *sarva-devamaya-guru,* the representative of all the demigods. So all the *prabhus* and *īśvaras* are ready to act according to your desire and Sn Caitanya Mahāprabhu's, which is to save all the fallen souls from the loathsome influence of the satanic personality of Kali and merge them deep in the ocean of love of God.

Our home, that "house in which the whole world can live," was in danger of being torn down because of our neglecting to properly clean it (by cleaning ourselves) and sheltering our little brothers and / or prospective guests. But, anyway, you will never allow your sincere and faithful sons to be evicted and the *saṅkīrtana* movement office of Lord Caitanya to be locked.

Because you are still living in your instructions with your real and sincere followers, we are being urged, by Providence's force, to start a big marathon of cleansing and rectifying the mistakes, in order to be able to fulfill your ideal of unity and purity, and that the world thirsty and hungry for Kṛṣṇa consciousness can be delivered, and the prophecy of Lord Caitanya fulfilled.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, may the light of your pure instructions always guide us on our path as we crawl through our spiritual childhood.

*Your eternal servants of ISKCON Rio de Janeiro, Brazil,*

## St. Louis

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

I met him at the "House of Flowers" in October of '72,  
a San Francisco pandal one thousand came to view.  
They gave me a Bhāgavatam, and one devotee said "Just see,  
help with a donation and your service will start for Prabhupāda's B.B.T."  
After reading that transcendental literature in 1973,  
I joined the ranks of the glorious saṅkīrtana party.  
My first day was as wonderful as wonderful could be,  
'cause the president sent me downtown to sell books for the B.B.T.  
In '74 we heard him speak in the L.A. temple,  
strongly preaching of deathless / less, giving the spiritual example.  
On Friday morning he said, "Distribute books, distribute books, distribute books, please."  
We were all convinced of a lifetime mission to serve his B.B. T.  
We all went to the airports in 1975,  
giving businessmen, servicemen, scientists, and scholars the chance for spiritual life.  
Armed with Bhāgavatam, Gītā, Kṛṣṇa book, and B.T.G.,  
it became known as the world's leading transcendental publishing house, Prabhupāda's B.B.T.  
By '76 the movement was spreading like wildfire on all fronts  
because of his steady determined effort of publishing so many books in so few months.  
He even stated that by distributing these books, all success there will be,  
even if everything else failed, keep the wheels turning for the B.B. T.  
Before he left in '77, our beloved Prabhupāda  
told of cultural conquest being the best way to spread the glory of God.  
By distribution of our books and Vedic culture, historians will one day see  
how the entire world was changed by the efforts of the B. B. T.  
Now it's ten years since he left us, almost to the day,  
and it's no surprise that many have left and gone astray.  
But all of us who are still around should hold on to the key  
of cooperating together for the purpose of maintaining and expanding the B.B.T.

He said that money was his blood, and his books were his heart,  
and if we kept on distributing, he really never would depart.  
So, Prabhupāda, by our menial efforts we beg to someday be  
sincere and dedicated servants of your B. B. T.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!*

(Written by Sūra dāsa)

## Salzburg

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

The sun is dawning, penetrating the darkness of the night.  
I hear your voice resounding in my ears and in my heart.  
You are present in pure transcendental sound penetrating my ears;  
You are present in your divine instructions, unchanged by years.  
I bathe your transcendental form with the fragrant oils of your total surrender to  
your guru. I bathe your transcendental form with fragrant, crystal clear waters of your  
unfathomable wisdom and knowledge. I bathe you with the love you have so kindly given me.  
By your mercy only may I serve you;  
By your mercy may I always remember you;  
By your mercy may I never forget you.

I dress you with the flowers of your boundless kindness.  
I decorate you with the garland of your unalloyed devotion.  
I eat you on the excellent seat of your unbreakable determination to save the fallen from eternal damnation.

I offer you the fragrant incense of your humility.  
I offer you the light of your eternal purity.  
I prostrate myself in the dust of your lotus feet and beg forgiveness for all my offenses.  
I hear your instructions resound in my ears, within my mind,  
my intelligence—awakening me, the soul, to eternal Kṛṣṇa consciousness.  
Thus the sound emanating from your lotus lips  
destroys the darkness born out of ignorance.

Tat very sound establishes me in devotional service.  
The secret of dharma is hidden within your heart,  
yet your heart is revealed within the pages of your books.  
Over centuries and millenniums let your heart beat,  
for you live forever in your books,  
as well as within the hearts of those who read them, live by them, and live to distribute them.  
The sun is dawning.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda and his devotees go to every town and village,  
spreading Lord Gaurāṅga's message: "Jīva Jāgo! Jīva Jāgo!"

*(Written by Jaya Gurudeva)*

*Your servants in Salzburg, Austria*

## San Diego

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In 1976 you wrote a letter to a disciple requesting him (and all of us) to not let our movement decrease, but at least to maintain it after your departure. At that time I distinctly remember thinking: "Why is Śrīla Prabhupāda simply asking us to maintain the movement after his departure? Previously he was always pushing us to expand. I thought maintenance was no problem at all. That is the least (or worst) we could do."

Now, eleven years later, I see my ignorance. I was under the impression that we (your disciples) were maintaining this movement, but factually we were doing nothing, and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, were doing everything. The movement was being maintained and expanded by you, but I foolishly thought we were qualified to do it.

I can also realize another aspect of your greatness by futher examining your request to maintain what you have given us. In that letter you said, " I've given you everything—temples, books, money, men, prestige, etc." You came, you gave us everything (because certainly we could not have achieved these things), and yet you were well aware that we had the capacity to gradually diminish and even destroy the institution. It seems to me that you were quite aware that you were leaving everything in the hands of incapable people. Otherwise, why at the time of incorporating the BBT did you mention that we are incorporating separately, in case ISKCON does not continue (so that at least the BBT continues)?

Certainly as Americans we are materially qualified in many ways. This you yourself told us on many occasions. Therefore we are capable of doing very wonderful service for Lord Caitanya. But still you were worried we would "spoil everything." This paradox is, however, resolved in your own statement to your Godbrother about your preaching successes, in which you said, "By Guru, Vaiṣṇava, and whatever position I've got, it is by Guru's mercy and the Vaiṣṇavas' blessings. Otherwise I am insignificant. By his \[Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s\] grace it has become a little successful; that's all. I have no credit. I do not know how things are happening, because I am not at all bona fide \[Vaiṣṇava\]. *Tadīyā vaiṣṇava sevā nistāra payecha kebā.* \["Without serving an ideal Vaiṣṇava, who can be delivered from the clutches of *māyā?"\]* I hope even though I do not live too many more years, I hope they will carry out his order."

Thus material qualifications will never prove to be our strength, but strict following of your instructions and humble dealings with the Vaiṣṇavas will be our success formula. Thus when you say "I hope they will carry out his order," the question is whether we will carry out your orders or will neglect them, misinterpret them, or simply forget that they exist.

It never ceases to amaze me how we become so easily diverted or even totally forgetful of your plain and simple instructions, whereas the instructions of your spiritual master remained perfectly intact Within your heart until you were able to fully execute them, by preaching in America, forty-five years after first receiving them. Your success formula is an open book: "People say I have done a wonderful thing. I do not know that I have done anything wonderful. But if I have any credit, it is that I have strictly followed the instructions of my Guru Mahārāja."

Thus maintaining the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and expanding it is simply a by-product of following your instructions. Your instructions are not difficult, but again they are the most difficult to follow, because to do so we have to become both unmotivated and humble.

We are praying for your mercy to awaken these qualifications in our heart so that we can follow your instruction to at least maintain this movement in your absence. But even when you were present, you gave so many indications that you had just barely begun your work. You chalked out plans for preaching for the next ten thousand years, giving unlimited engagement for your disciples and generations of granddisciples. Falling at your feet, we beg to become your instruments in the continuing expansion of your mercy throughout this planet. Let every desire you ever expressed find a place in our hearts.

(Written by Mahātmā dāsa)

*Your servants in New Govardhana (San Diego),*

## San Francisco

"The activities and characteristics of the ethereal element can be observed as accommodation for the room for the external and internal existences of all living entities, namely the field of activities of the vital air, the senses, and the mind."

"This verse is the potential basis for great scientific research work, for it explains how subtle forms are generated from the ethereal element, what their characteristics and action are, and how the tangible elements, namely air, fire, water, and earth, are manifested from the subtle form." *(Bhag.* 3.26.34& purport)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Śrī-Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. At great personal expense, you are delivering this *saṅkīrtana* movement to so many fallen conditioned souls. Being somehow or other engaged in your service, we are continually experiencing your mercy. Especially when we are involved in the scientific preaching work of the Bhaktivedanta Institute, everything goes on quite well. We only hope that Your Divine Grace will be kind enough to send more devotees to help broadcast the nectar of this scientific and scholarly preaching to the conditioned souls: devotees who are more pure Vaiṣṇavas than ourselves and even materially more qualified, with Ph.D.s, Dr. Sc.s, LL.D.s, and so forth.

In this endeavor to establish a background for *varṇāśrama-dharma* and thus make *bhakti-yoga* accessible to everyone, preaching to the currently recognized intellectuals and research scientists is most essential and effective. There may be special qualifications required to perform scientific preaching (just as there are special qualifications for being a cook, mother, king, or *pūjārī*), but the association of pure devotees in a *saṅkīrtana* party somehow gets the work done. Here in San Francisco, the devotees are engaged in many activities of service, art, and culture. We are hoping that by these activities we will contribute to your *saṅkīrtana* movement to the greatest possible extent.

On this Vyāsa-pūjā day, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to thank you for the process *of bhakti-yoga,* for admission into your association, and for the company of all the devotees of Lord Nityānanda and Lord Caitanya. As we mature, we realize how easy it is to fall victim to *māyā* without your physical presence. We pray that devotees all over the world can assist in the preaching work of the Bhaktivedanta Institute and thereby stimulate the development of brahminical culture and *varṇāśrama-dharma.* We thank you for the association of so many different kinds of Godbrothers and Godsisters, who all contribute with love, humility, and determination the variety of services needed to make a *yātrā* a success. And we promise to rededicate ourselves in the coming year to chanting the holy names of the Pañca-tattva and Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, to studying *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Bhagavad-gītā,* etc., and to controlling our senses more and more firmly in your service.

*Your spiritual children,*

## San Salvador

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this glorious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we offer our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you for having appeared in this mortal world to fulfill the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu: *yāre dekha, tāre kaha 'kṛṣṇa'-upadeśa.* You have appeared to spread the holy name of Kṛṣṇa throughout the world, and we are greatly fortunate to have had your physical association and to be left with your divine instructions as we try to continue your preaching mission. How fortunate we are is confirmed in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta—brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva guru kṛṣṇa prasāde pāya bhakti latā-bīja:* "Out of millions of wandering living entities, one who is very fortunate gets the opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa." Due to our long association with material nature, our life was dark like night, but out of your limitless causeless mercy you have given us a chance to achieve spiritual maturity.

On this auspicious day when we celebrate your appearance in the material world, we desire to invoke Kṛṣṇa's grace, so that within our hearts we can gain the ability to understand your transcendental qualities and how you are spreading the name of Kṛṣṇa all over the world.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us your causeless mercy so that we can become pure devotees and propagate Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement all over the world.

*Your unworthy servants from New Kurukṣetra Mandira, San Salvador,*

Mahābhāgavata dāsa Goswami

## Sao Jose

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Your Divine Grace, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Pārivrājakācārya Aṣṭotaraśata Śrī Śrīmad A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, on your auspicious appearance day.

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept these simple and humble words in homage to your divine appearance.  
Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, how wonderful was your appearance in the material world! How successful your mission!  
Your Divine Grace could understand with your perfect intelligence the mission of your spiritual master.  
You surrendered yourself in body, mind, and words. You gave your life to realize the order of your spiritual master.  
O beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your mercy Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement has reached around the world, fulfilling the prediction of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura.  
O beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, I can't glorify you properly. On account of this I beg your mercy upon this conditioned soul.  
You cried out very loudly, "Go out and preach." This fulfills our life and awards us spiritual realization.  
Now every one of us has the duty, the obligation, to become a mature devotee, and spiritual maturity means to always fulfill your orders and never to abandon ISKCON, which Your Divine Grace brought from Kṛṣṇaloka.  
O beloved Vaiṣṇava, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have Kṛṣṇa, and you have the power to give Him to every one of us.

(Written by Nanda-kumāra dāsa)

*We pray at your divine lotus feet for your mercy and beg to remain your servants,*

Nanda-kumāra dāsa / Priyadṛṣṭi dāsa / Jayantī-devī dāsī /

## Scotland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We beg to offer our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

By your mercy a temple is now established in Scotland again, as was your desire. A humble beginning, but soon by your mercy everything will increase. The devotees are young and have a lot of energy to give, so we'll distribute your books—it's the only way to live.

We would like to make this humble offering in appreciation of the most important gift you gave the world, *YOUR BOOKS.* Please look kindly upon us, for we are so imperfect, and our offering is not really worthy of offering at your lotus feet. But nevertheless we feel by our attempt and your kindness and causeless mercy that we will become perfect.

The books that you gave us:  
eternal wisdom sublime.  
They will light up the way  
for all souls of this time.

The books that you gave us:  
establish the Absolute Truth  
and direct us how from māyā’s allurements  
we can become aloof.

The books that you gave us:  
knowledge of Kṛṣṇa on each page;  
the positive way out of illusion  
in this quarrelsome age.

The books that you gave us:  
for all fallen souls of this age.  
How we must distribute them,  
your pleasure our wage.

The books that you gave us:  
we must distribute everywhere.  
Each fallen soul should get one;  
not one jīva should we spare.

The books that you gave us:  
for every home in the land,  
so that all fallen souls  
can always have Kṛṣṇa at hand.

The books that you gave us:  
our heart and our soul  
prostrated at your feet,  
your pleasure our goal.

Please always engage us in your book distribution.

*Your insignificant servants of the Scottish Yātrā,*

## Seattle

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your holy feet. All glories to your spiritual master. His Divine Grace Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja Prabhupāda, whose mission you accepted as your life's work.

All over the world one can find people who believe that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead and that you are His pure devotee who came to teach and engage us in the Lord's service. You said that establishing *varṇāśrama* was the next big step in your mission. It seems then that not everyone will be living in a temple, surrendering to temple authority. Indeed, if Kṛṣṇa consciousness is going to spread as Caitanya Mahāprabhu predicted, there will not be just a temple in every town and village, but in every home a temple. Let us unpresumptuously build our own confidence and march on to what you hoped we would achieve—spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness to every town and village.

*Your servants in Seattle,*

## Seawell Village

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, we feel completely empty, for there is nothing of worth to offer you since you yourself have given us everything. No one within the universe can sufficiently praise you for establishing ISKCON all over the world. You had said that ISKCON is your body. From this we can understand that the more that you are kept in the center of ISKCON, the more ISKCON will expand all over the globe. If this supreme position of yours is even slightly tampered with, it may lead to havoc for your transcendental society. All the current threats to ISKCON may be seen as your warnings (and in a sense your mercy) to bring us to our senses and realization that ISKCON cannot be separated from you.

All credit is due to you alone, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We have never been able to get the mercy of your personal association, but we were first introduced to you through your books. Since then, our relationship with you has developed by the help of our spiritual masters, your good sons, who spearhead preaching in Guyana. Their only business is to inject pure love for you and Kṛṣṇa and to bring everyone to your lotus feet and thus to the lotus feet of the Lord.

We want to always participate in your *saṅkīrtana* movement, and we pray not to dare misuse your ISKCON for any selfish, personal motive. We hope that somehow we will gradually come to the platform of constantly remembering you as our eternal lord and master.

Your life on this planet is an open book, within which is contained the answers to all difficulties faced by conditioned souls. Whenever a seemingly hopeless problem arises, one can turn to the relevant chapter of your life and become solaced. You conclusively demonstrated how a worldwide movement can be inaugurated by purity and determination in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and how these factors are more powerful than the dollar.

Although Guyana is probably the most economically unstable country in the world, by the powerful preaching of your sincere sons your movement is welcomed and embraced by so many. Because they maintain your pure standard and are free from ulterior motives, all their endeavors are bearing fruit. We have faith that simply by following your instructions everything will be bestowed on us. Originally at New Pānihāṭi we had merely a piece of virgin land and your books. Then you sent us a few men, then more devotees and increasing congregation. Then, by your transcendental arrangement, our spiritual masters, your sons, came into our life. Then came a simple temple and the beginning of *gurukula.* Last year you sent Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulacandra, the life and soul of everyone here. This year you are inspiring us to perform *pada-yātrā* throughout the country, introduce an increased feast program, a new preaching center, a Vedic day school. New vehicles are being acquired for preaching. And so on ...

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know that you are both the forefront and the background of all these expansions. Things will further expand, unlimitedly, if you see that we are dedicated to following your instructions and remaining pure. Otherwise, everything gradually collapses.

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, we beg you to guide us so that we will never devīate in our understanding of Your Divine Grace.

(Written by Paramatma dāsa)

*Your fallen servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulacandra Mandira, New Pānihāṭi, Berbice Guyana*

## Seoul

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All of humanity is indebted to you for the gifts you have given. The confidential knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness was known and practiced only within one part of the world. Even though some parts of the *Vedas* were studied by Western scholars, they had no idea what the *Vedas* actually meant. Only after you came, as an ambassador from the spiritual world, did the knowledge of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* spread and change the hearts of the conditioned souls. Your activities surpass even those of great persons described in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.* Who else in history has traveled throughout the world converting the most fallen people into Vaiṣṇavas?

You set the example and laid the foundation for an entire spiritual culture under the banner of ISKCON. By your vigorous preaching, "Hare Kṛṣṇa" has become a household word in many countries. But by your mercy, there is no lack of engagement, as there are still millions of people who have never heard of Kṛṣṇa. So, as your children and grandchildren, it's our duty to take up your mission and cooperate to push forward Kṛṣṇa consciousness with great enthusiasm. On this auspicious day of your divine appearance, we fervently pray for your blessings as we begin to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Korea, a land of dog-eaters. As a new twig on the *bhakti* tree of Lord Caitanya, we are depending on you for our sustenance, strength, and inspiration.

*Your servants,*  
Jaya Baladeva dāsa / Ācārya dāsa / Bhakta Jay Young Park / Bhakta In Suk Oh

## Singapore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your transcendental self. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Kindly do excuse these fallen souls for any offenses. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the supreme teacher, the most bona fide spiritual master. The more we try to read the *śāstras,* the more we can understand your qualities. You showed us by your practical behavior how magnanimous you are. Many of us tried to take you lightly or cheaply, thinking that we too could be like you, but we only failed very miserably.

Kindly do excuse us for all the numerous offenses that we have committed unto you. We are humbly praying for your mercy, so as to somehow or other carry on this mission of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Sundara-gopāla dāsa

## Soviet Union

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this holy day of your appearance,  
We would like to offer you a report  
From a place your movement meets great interference  
And many devotees are convicted in court.

We are referring to the USSR,  
Where there is found much agony and ecstasy.  
Under the hammer, sickle, and the Red star,  
Spreading your movement is an emergency.

The demons there are very cruel and strong.  
They don't tolerate religion, scriptures, or God.  
Such a condition cannot last very long,  
Because many Soviets consider Communism odd.  
A revolution is resulting from reading your books.  
It is confirmed they are in great demand.  
If the devotees avoid the KGB 's looks,  
For each book they find fifty rubles in their hand.

Knowing your great love for book distribution  
They are smuggled, photocopied, and printed,  
All at great risk of the State's prosecution,  
New ways of saṅkīrtana are invented.

In the Soviet Union your books are forbidden.  
One devotee suffered great torture and pain  
To reveal the location of where they were hidden.  
He endured, and the books the police did not obtain.

Along with the Gītā there is now Kṛṣṇa book  
In the language the Soviets read.  
Many are anxious just to get a good look,  
And when they do, from illusion they 're freed.  
The more the KGB applies its pressure  
To smash your movement to pieces,  
Many more Russians begin to take pleasure  
In your instructions; thus the movement increases.

Now in prison there are more than twenty-five.  
They deserve our help and assistance  
To keep their faith in Lord Kṛṣṇa alive  
Because they face such fierce resistance.

You wanted us to think of the world first,  
Then, of our local preaching field.  
In order to avoid the absolute worst,  
To everyone we make this appeal:

If the Soviet government understands  
That no one in the world is concerned  
For the devotees they have clutched in their hands,  
They will squeeze them harder, we have learned.

Now is the time for our movement to unite  
Just as Caitanya and His followers did  
In front of the Kazi’s house that night.  
Then saṅkīrtana the Kazi did not forbid.

We have the example of Mahāprabhu  
When someone tries to stop His mission.  
Congregate and demonstrate is what to do,  
In order to fulfill the Lord's ambition.

When the Soviets see there is huge support  
For the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement everywhere,  
To the KGB, Gorbachev will make a report:  
"From the prisons, let them out of there."

Making the Russians into devotees of the Lord  
Can make this planet's awful fate change.  
This fact is so true it should not be ignored,  
And it is up to us to arrange.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so merciful and kind  
For letting us deliver your teaching.  
In this world it is impossible to find  
Anything better to do than preaching.

*Your humble, struggling servants inside and outside the Soviet Union,*
